# Russia's War on Ukraine: Material Quotes, 15 November 2026 to 28 November 2026

Digest for 28 November 2026. Period: 2026-11-15 to 2026-11-28 (14 days, by publication date, UTC). 1124 current quotes from BBC News, The Kyiv Independent, Reuters; 18 older quotes hidden as superseded.

Quotes are copied verbatim from the source articles. Section summaries are generated by an LLM.

## War Intensity & Battlefield

*LLM summary:* News quotes indicate an intensification of Russia's war on Ukraine, characterized by massive drone and missile strikes targeting critical energy and transport infrastructure, as well as Ukrainian retaliation through long-range drone attacks on Russian oil facilities and logistics networks.

## Conflicting Parties' Resources

*LLM summary:* News reports indicate that Russia's war on Ukraine has resulted in massive casualties and extensive military equipment losses on both sides, with Russian losses significantly surpassing those of Ukraine. Meanwhile, Russia relies on foreign military and component supplies, while Ukraine depends heavily on international financial and military support.

## Domestic Politics & Public Opinion

*LLM summary:* While a majority of Ukrainians express long-term confidence in victory and willingness to endure the war, high-level corruption investigations have shaken Ukrainian politics. Concurrently, Russia has intensified domestic repressions and increased penalties for anti-war dissent.
